            </div>
            <input v-model="filters.search" @input="onSearchInput" type="text" placeholder="Buscar por código, descripción..." class="w-full pl-12 pr-10 py-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700 placeholder-gray-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#611717] focus:border-transparent focus:bg-white transition-all duration-200" />
            <button v-if="filters.search" @click="limpiarBusqueda" class="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-4 flex items-center text-gray-400 hover:text-[#611717] transition-colors" title="Limpiar búsqueda">
              <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" /></svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Contador -->
      <div v-if="!loading && !error" class="mb-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
        Mostrando <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ documentos.length }}</strong> de <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ total }}</strong> proyectos
        <span v-if="filters.search"> para "<strong>{{ filters.search }}</strong>"</span>
      </div>

      <!-- Loading -->
      <div v-if="loading" class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm py-16 text-center">
        <div class="animate-spin rounded-full h-12 w-12 border-b-2 border-[#611717] mx-auto"></div>
        <p class="mt-4 text-gray-600 text-sm">Cargando proyectos de ley...</p>
      </div>

      <!-- Error -->
      <div v-else-if="error" class="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-6 text-center">
        <p class="text-red-700 font-medium text-sm">{{ error }}</p>
        <button @click="cargarDocumentos" class="mt-3 px-4 py-2 bg-[#611717] text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-[#3a060d] text-sm">Reintentar</button>
      </div>

      <!-- Sin resultados -->
      <div v-else-if="documentos.length === 0" class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm py-16 text-center">
        <div class="text-5xl mb-3">📭</div>
        <h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-800 mb-1">No hay proyectos de ley</h3>
        <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm">{{ filters.search ? 'Prueba con otros términos de búsqueda.' : 'Aún no hay proyectos registrados.' }}</p>
      </div>

      <!-- Tabla -->
      <div v-else class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-md border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
        <div class="overflow-x-auto">
          <table class="min-w-full text-sm">
            <thead class="bg-[#611717] text-white">
              <tr>
                <!-- Nueva Columna Nº -->
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Nº</th>
                <!-- Columna Código (antes Nº) -->
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Código</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Fecha</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Descripción</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Proyectista</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-center text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Adjunto</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody class="divide-y divide-gray-100">
              <tr v-for="(doc, index) in documentos" :key="doc._id" class="hover:bg-[#fbf8f1] transition-colors">
                <!-- Nº: Numeración global (Total al 1) -->
                <td class="px-4 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                  <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#fbf8f1] text-[#611717] font-bold text-xs border border-[#e3d194]">
                    {{ total - ((page - 1) * itemsPorPagina) - index }}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <!-- Código (antes Nº) -->
                <td class="px-4 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                  <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#fbf8f1] text-[#611717] font-bold text-xs border border-[#e3d194]">{{ doc.numero }}</span>
                </td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-gray-700 whitespace-nowrap text-xs">{{ formatearFecha(doc.fechaIngreso) }}</td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-gray-700 min-w-[320px] max-w-md">
                  <div class="line-clamp-3 text-justify text-xs leading-relaxed" :title="doc.asunto">{{ doc.asunto }}</div>
                </td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-gray-700 min-w-[180px] text-xs">
                  <span v-if="Array.isArray(doc.peticionario)">{{ doc.peticionario.join(', ') }}</span>
                  <span v-else>{{ doc.peticionario || '—' }}</span>
                </td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                  <a v-if="doc.archivo && doc.archivo.url" :href="doc.archivo.url" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 border-2 border-[#611717] text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#611717] hover:text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all duration-200" title="Ver PDF">
                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" /></svg>
                    Ver PDF
                  </a>
                  <span v-else class="text-gray-300 text-xs">Sin archivo</span>
                </td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <!-- Paginación -->
        <div v-if="totalPages > 1" class="px-6 py-5 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-4 bg-[#fbf8f1]">
          <div class="text-xs text-gray-600">
            Mostrando registros del <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ (page - 1) * itemsPorPagina + 1 }}</strong> al <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ Math.min(page * itemsPorPagina, total) }}</strong> de un total de <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ total }}</strong> registros
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button @click="prevPage" :disabled="page === 1" class="p-2 rounded-md text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#e3d194] disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors">
              <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7" /></svg>
            </button>
            <button v-for="p in paginasVisibles" :key="p" @click="irAPagina(p)" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition-colors" :class="page === p ? 'bg-[#611717] text-white shadow-md' : 'hover:bg-[#e3d194] text-[#611717]'">{{ p }}</button>
            <span v-if="totalPages > 5 && page < totalPages - 2" class="px-1 text-[#611717]">...</span>
            <button @click="nextPage" :disabled="page === totalPages" class="p-2 rounded-md text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#e3d194] disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors">
              <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" /></svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'

useHead({
  title: 'Proyectos de Ley Rechazados | Senado de Bolivia',
  meta: [{ name: 'description', content: 'Listado de proyectos de ley rechazados por la Cámara de Senadores.' }]
})

const config = useRuntimeConfig()
const API_BASE_URL = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

const documentos = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref(null)
const page = ref(1)
const totalPages = ref(1)
const total = ref(0)
const itemsPorPagina = 10
const filters = ref({ search: '' })
let searchTimeout = null

const paginasVisibles = computed(() => {
  const paginas = []
  const rango = 2
  for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages.value; i++) {
    if (i === 1 || i === totalPages.value || (i >= page.value - rango && i <= page.value + rango)) { paginas.push(i) }
  }
  return paginas
})

const formatearFecha = (fecha) => {
  if (!fecha) return '—'
  try {
    const d = new Date(fecha)
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—'
    return d.toLocaleDateString('es-BO', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' })
  } catch { return '—' }
}

const cargarDocumentos = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = null
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    params.append('page', String(page.value))
    params.append('limit', String(itemsPorPagina))
    if (filters.value.search) params.append('search', filters.value.search)

    const url = `${API_BASE_URL}/legislativos/proyecto-ley-rechazados?${params.toString()}`
    const response = await fetch(url)
    const result = await response.json()

    if (!response.ok || !result.success) throw new Error(result.message || 'Error al cargar')

    documentos.value = result.data?.documentos || []
    total.value = result.data?.total || 0
    totalPages.value = result.data?.pages || 1
  } catch (err) {
    error.value = err.message
    documentos.value = []
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const onSearchInput = () => {
  if (searchTimeout) clearTimeout(searchTimeout)
  searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => { page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }, 500)
}

const limpiarBusqueda = () => { filters.value.search = ''; page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }

const irAPagina = (p) => {
  if (p >= 1 && p <= totalPages.value) { page.value = p; cargarDocumentos(); if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }) }
}

const prevPage = () => { if (page.value > 1) irAPagina(page.value - 1) }
const nextPage = () => { if (page.value < totalPages.value) irAPagina(page.value + 1) }

onMounted(() => { cargarDocumentos() })
</script>

<style scoped>
.line-clamp-3 { display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 3; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden; }
</style>

          Volver al inicio
        </NuxtLink>
      </div>

      <!-- Encabezado -->
      <div class="mb-6">
        <div class="inline-block bg-[#611717] text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full mb-3 uppercase tracking-wider">

          <div class="relative flex-1">
            <div class="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-4 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
              <svg class="w-5 h-5 text-[#611717]" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z" /></svg>
            </div>
            <input v-model="filters.search" @input="onSearchInput" type="text" placeholder="Buscar por código, descripción..." class="w-full pl-12 pr-10 py-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700 placeholder-gray-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#611717] focus:border-transparent focus:bg-white transition-all duration-200" />
            <button v-if="filters.search" @click="limpiarBusqueda" class="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-4 flex items-center text-gray-400 hover:text-[#611717] transition-colors" title="Limpiar búsqueda">
              <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" /></svg>

        <span v-if="filters.search"> para "<strong>{{ filters.search }}</strong>"</span>
        
        <!-- 🔥 AQUÍ SE MUESTRA EL PERIODO GLOBAL (enviado por el backend) -->
        <span class="ml-2 text-xs text-gray-400" v-if="rangoGlobal">
          · Periodo: {{ rangoGlobal }}
        </span>
      </div>

      <!-- Loading -->
      <div v-if="loading" class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm py-16 text-center">
        <div class="animate-spin rounded-full h-12 w-12 border-b-2 border-[#611717] mx-auto"></div>
        <p class="mt-4 text-gray-600 text-sm">Cargando proyectos de ley...</p>
      </div>

      <!-- Error -->
      <div v-else-if="error" class="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-6 text-center">
        <p class="text-red-700 font-medium text-sm">{{ error }}</p>
        <button @click="cargarDocumentos" class="mt-3 px-4 py-2 bg-[#611717] text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-[#3a060d] text-sm">Reintentar</button>
      </div>

      <!-- Sin resultados -->
      <div v-else-if="documentos.length === 0" class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm py-16 text-center">
        <div class="text-5xl mb-3">📭</div>
        <h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-800 mb-1">No hay proyectos de ley</h3>
        <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm">{{ filters.search ? 'Prueba con otros términos de búsqueda.' : 'Aún no hay proyectos registrados.' }}</p>
      </div>

      <!-- Tabla -->
      <div v-else class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-md border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
        <div class="overflow-x-auto">
          <table class="min-w-full text-sm">
            <thead class="bg-[#611717] text-white">
              <tr>
                <!-- 🔥 BOTÓN Nº PARA INVERTIR EL ORDEN GLOBAL -->
                <th 
                  @click="toggleOrden"
                  class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer select-none hover:bg-[#7a1f1f] transition-colors group"
                  title="Click para invertir el orden"
                >
                  <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                    Nº
                    <span class="text-[10px] opacity-80 group-hover:opacity-100">
                      {{ ordenDescendente ? '▼' : '▲' }}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                </th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Código</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Fecha</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Descripción</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-center text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Adjunto</th>

            Mostrando registros del <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ (page - 1) * itemsPorPagina + 1 }}</strong> al <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ Math.min(page * itemsPorPagina, total) }}</strong> de un total de <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ total }}</strong> registros
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button @click="prevPage" :disabled="page === 1" class="p-2 rounded-md text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#e3d194] disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors">

            </button>

              <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" /></svg>
            </button>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'

useHead({
  title: 'Proyectos de Ley en Revisión | Senado de Bolivia',
  meta: [{ name: 'description', content: 'Listado de proyectos de ley en revisión originados en la Cámara de Diputados.' }]
})

const config = useRuntimeConfig()
const API_BASE_URL = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

const documentos = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref(null)
const page = ref(1)
const totalPages = ref(1)
const total = ref(0)
const itemsPorPagina = 10
const filters = ref({ search: '' })
let searchTimeout = null

const ordenDescendente = ref(true)

// 🔥 Variables para guardar las fechas enviadas por el BACKEND
const fechaExtremaMax = ref(null)
const fechaExtremaMin = ref(null)

const paginasVisibles = computed(() => {
  const paginas = []
  const rango = 2
  for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages.value; i++) {
    if (i === 1 || i === totalPages.value || (i >= page.value - rango && i <= page.value + rango)) { paginas.push(i) }
  }
  return paginas
})

// 🔥 Texto que muestra el periodo (AHORA VIENE DEL BACKEND)
const rangoGlobal = computed(() => {
  if (!fechaExtremaMax.value && !fechaExtremaMin.value) return null

  const formatear = (fechaStr) => {
    if (!fechaStr) return ''
    const fecha = new Date(fechaStr)
    if (isNaN(fecha.getTime())) return ''
    return fecha.toLocaleDateString('es-BO', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' })
  }

  const fMax = formatear(fechaExtremaMax.value)
  const fMin = formatear(fechaExtremaMin.value)

  if (fMax && fMin) {
    // Si el orden es descendente (más nuevo primero), mostramos de Mayor a Menor. 
    // Si es ascendente, de Menor a Mayor.
    return ordenDescendente.value ? `${fMax} – ${fMin}` : `${fMin} – ${fMax}`
  }
  return null
})

const formatearFecha = (fecha) => {
  if (!fecha) return '—'
  try {
    const d = new Date(fecha)
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—'
    return d.toLocaleDateString('es-BO', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' })
  } catch { return '—' }
}

const numeroSecuencial = (index) => {
  if (ordenDescendente.value) {
    return total.value - ((page.value - 1) * itemsPorPagina) - index
  }
  return (page.value - 1) * itemsPorPagina + index + 1
}

const cargarDocumentos = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = null
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    params.append('page', String(page.value))
    params.append('limit', String(itemsPorPagina))
    if (filters.value.search) params.append('search', filters.value.search)
    params.append('order', ordenDescendente.value ? 'desc' : 'asc')

    const url = `${API_BASE_URL}/legislativos/proyecto-ley-revision-diputados?${params.toString()}`
    const response = await fetch(url)
    const result = await response.json()

    if (!response.ok || !result.success) throw new Error(result.message || 'Error al cargar')

    documentos.value = result.data?.documentos || []
    total.value = result.data?.total || 0
    totalPages.value = result.data?.pages || 1

    // 🔥 GUARDAMOS LAS FECHAS EXTREMAS GLOBALES QUE ENVÍA EL BACKEND
    fechaExtremaMax.value = result.data?.fechaMaxima || null
    fechaExtremaMin.value = result.data?.fechaMinima || null

  } catch (err) {
    error.value = err.message
    documentos.value = []
    fechaExtremaMax.value = null
    fechaExtremaMin.value = null
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const toggleOrden = () => {
  if (loading.value) return
  ordenDescendente.value = !ordenDescendente.value
  page.value = 1
  cargarDocumentos()
  if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })
}

const onSearchInput = () => {
  if (searchTimeout) clearTimeout(searchTimeout)
  searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => { page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }, 500)
}

const limpiarBusqueda = () => { filters.value.search = ''; page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }

const irAPagina = (p) => {
  if (p >= 1 && p <= totalPages.value) { 
    page.value = p
    cargarDocumentos()
    if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }) 
  }
}

const prevPage = () => { if (page.value > 1) irAPagina(page.value - 1) }
const nextPage = () => { if (page.value < totalPages.value) irAPagina(page.value + 1) }

onMounted(() => { cargarDocumentos() })
</script>

pages\proyectos-sancionados.vue

              <svg class="w-5 h-5 text-[#611717]" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z" /></svg>
            </div>
            <input v-model="filters.search" @input="onSearchInput" type="text" placeholder="Buscar por código, descripción..." class="w-full pl-12 pr-10 py-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700 placeholder-gray-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#611717] focus:border-transparent focus:bg-white transition-all duration-200" />
            <button v-if="filters.search" @click="limpiarBusqueda" class="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-4 flex items-center text-gray-400 hover:text-[#611717] transition-colors" title="Limpiar búsqueda">
              <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" /></svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Contador -->
      <div v-if="!loading && !error" class="mb-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
        Mostrando <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ documentos.length }}</strong> de <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ total }}</strong> proyectos
        <span v-if="filters.search"> para "<strong>{{ filters.search }}</strong>"</span>
      </div>

      <!-- Loading -->
      <div v-if="loading" class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm py-16 text-center">
        <div class="animate-spin rounded-full h-12 w-12 border-b-2 border-[#611717] mx-auto"></div>
        <p class="mt-4 text-gray-600 text-sm">Cargando proyectos de ley...</p>

        <button @click="cargarDocumentos" class="mt-3 px-4 py-2 bg-[#611717] text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-[#3a060d] text-sm">Reintentar</button>
      </div>

      <!-- Sin resultados -->
      <div v-else-if="documentos.length === 0" class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm py-16 text-center">
        <div class="text-5xl mb-3">📭</div>
        <h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-800 mb-1">No hay proyectos de ley</h3>
        <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm">{{ filters.search ? 'Prueba con otros términos de búsqueda.' : 'Aún no hay proyectos registrados.' }}</p>
      </div>

      <!-- Tabla -->
      <div v-else class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-md border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
        <div class="overflow-x-auto">
          <table class="min-w-full text-sm">
            <thead class="bg-[#611717] text-white">
              <tr>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Nº</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Fecha de Petición</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Descripción</th>

                </td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-gray-700 whitespace-nowrap text-xs">{{ formatearFecha(doc.fechaIngreso) }}</td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-gray-700 min-w-[320px] max-w-md">
                  <div class="line-clamp-3 text-justify text-xs leading-relaxed" :title="doc.asunto">{{ doc.asunto }}</div>
                </td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-gray-700 min-w-[180px] text-xs">
                  <span v-if="Array.isArray(doc.peticionario)">{{ doc.peticionario.join(', ') }}</span>
                  <span v-else>{{ doc.peticionario || '—' }}</span>
                </td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                  <a v-if="doc.archivo && doc.archivo.url" :href="doc.archivo.url" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 border-2 border-[#611717] text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#611717] hover:text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all duration-200" title="Ver PDF">
                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" /></svg>
                    Ver PDF
                  </a>
                  <span v-else class="text-gray-300 text-xs">Sin archivo</span>
                </td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <!-- Paginación -->
        <div v-if="totalPages > 1" class="px-6 py-5 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-4 bg-[#fbf8f1]">
          <div class="text-xs text-gray-600">
            Mostrando registros del <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ (page - 1) * itemsPorPagina + 1 }}</strong> al <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ Math.min(page * itemsPorPagina, total) }}</strong> de un total de <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ total }}</strong> registros
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button @click="prevPage" :disabled="page === 1" class="p-2 rounded-md text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#e3d194] disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors">
              <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7" /></svg>
            </button>
            <button v-for="p in paginasVisibles" :key="p" @click="irAPagina(p)" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition-colors" :class="page === p ? 'bg-[#611717] text-white shadow-md' : 'hover:bg-[#e3d194] text-[#611717]'">{{ p }}</button>
            <span v-if="totalPages > 5 && page < totalPages - 2" class="px-1 text-[#611717]">...</span>
            <button @click="nextPage" :disabled="page === totalPages" class="p-2 rounded-md text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#e3d194] disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors">
              <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" /></svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'

useHead({
  title: 'Proyectos de Ley Sancionados | Senado de Bolivia',
  meta: [{ name: 'description', content: 'Listado de proyectos de ley sancionados por la Cámara de Senadores.' }]
})

const config = useRuntimeConfig()
const API_BASE_URL = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

const documentos = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref(null)
const page = ref(1)
const totalPages = ref(1)
const total = ref(0)
const itemsPorPagina = 10
const filters = ref({ search: '' })
let searchTimeout = null

const paginasVisibles = computed(() => {
  const paginas = []
  const rango = 2
  for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages.value; i++) {
    if (i === 1 || i === totalPages.value || (i >= page.value - rango && i <= page.value + rango)) { paginas.push(i) }
  }
  return paginas
})

const formatearFecha = (fecha) => {
  if (!fecha) return '—'
  try {
    const d = new Date(fecha)
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—'
    return d.toLocaleDateString('es-BO', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' })
  } catch { return '—' }
}

const cargarDocumentos = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = null
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    params.append('page', String(page.value))
    params.append('limit', String(itemsPorPagina))
    if (filters.value.search) params.append('search', filters.value.search)

    const url = `${API_BASE_URL}/legislativos/proyecto-ley-sancionados?${params.toString()}`
    const response = await fetch(url)
    const result = await response.json()

    if (!response.ok || !result.success) throw new Error(result.message || 'Error al cargar')

    documentos.value = result.data?.documentos || []
    total.value = result.data?.total || 0
    totalPages.value = result.data?.pages || 1
  } catch (err) {
    error.value = err.message
    documentos.value = []
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const onSearchInput = () => {
  if (searchTimeout) clearTimeout(searchTimeout)
  searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => { page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }, 500)
}

const limpiarBusqueda = () => { filters.value.search = ''; page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }

const irAPagina = (p) => {
  if (p >= 1 && p <= totalPages.value) { page.value = p; cargarDocumentos(); if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }) }
}

const prevPage = () => { if (page.value > 1) irAPagina(page.value - 1) }
const nextPage = () => { if (page.value < totalPages.value) irAPagina(page.value + 1) }

onMounted(() => { cargarDocumentos() })
</script>

<style scoped>
.line-clamp-3 { display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 3; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden; }
</style>

                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider min-w-[500px]">Descripción</th>

            <span v-if="totalPages > 5 && page < totalPages - 2" class="px-1 text-[#611717]">...</span>

              <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" /></svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'

useHead({
  title: 'Proyectos de Ley en Tratamiento | Senado de Bolivia',
  meta: [{ name: 'description', content: 'Listado de proyectos de ley en tratamiento originados en la Cámara de Senadores.' }]
})

const config = useRuntimeConfig()
const API_BASE_URL = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

const documentos = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref(null)
const page = ref(1)
const totalPages = ref(1)
const total = ref(0)
const itemsPorPagina = 10
const filters = ref({ search: '' })
let searchTimeout = null

// 🔥 Estado del ordenamiento (true = descendente / false = ascendente)
const ordenDescendente = ref(true)

const paginasVisibles = computed(() => {
  const paginas = []
  const rango = 2
  for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages.value; i++) {
    if (i === 1 || i === totalPages.value || (i >= page.value - rango && i <= page.value + rango)) { paginas.push(i) }
  }
  return paginas
})

// 🔥 Función procesadora Avanzada para Nombres
const formatearProyectista = (peticionario) => {
  if (!peticionario) return ['—']
  
  // 1. Unir si viene como Array
  let texto = Array.isArray(peticionario) ? peticionario.join(' ') : peticionario

  // 2. Agregar un delimitador (|) justo antes de "Sen.", "Senador", "Ex. Sen.", etc.
  // Esto separa los nombres aunque vengan pegados y sin comas en el texto.
  let textoFormateado = texto.replace(/(Ex\.?\s*Sen\.?|Ex\s*Senador|Sen\.?|Senador|Senadora)(?=\s[A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑ])/gi, '|$1')

  // 3. Separar por el delimitador (|) o por comas
  return textoFormateado
    .split(/[|,]/) // Separar por el pipe o por comas
    .map(nombre => {
      // 4. Limpiar el nombre quitando los prefijos "Sen.", "Ex. Sen.", etc.
      let limpio = nombre.replace(/^(Ex\.?\s*Sen\.?|Ex\s*Senador|Sen\.?|Senador|Senadora)\s+/gi, '')
      return limpio.trim()
    })
    .filter(nombre => nombre.length > 0) // Quitar vacíos
}

// 🔥 Numeración secuencial GLOBAL basada en el orden actual
const numeroSecuencial = (index) => {
  if (ordenDescendente.value) {
    return total.value - ((page.value - 1) * itemsPorPagina) - index
  }
  return (page.value - 1) * itemsPorPagina + index + 1
}

const cargarDocumentos = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = null
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    params.append('page', String(page.value))
    params.append('limit', String(itemsPorPagina))
    if (filters.value.search) params.append('search', filters.value.search)
    
    // 🔥 Enviamos el orden dinámico al backend
    params.append('order', ordenDescendente.value ? 'desc' : 'asc')

    const url = `${API_BASE_URL}/legislativos/proyecto-ley-tratamiento-senado?${params.toString()}`
    const response = await fetch(url)
    const result = await response.json()

    if (!response.ok || !result.success) throw new Error(result.message || 'Error al cargar')

    documentos.value = result.data?.documentos || []
    total.value = result.data?.total || 0
    totalPages.value = result.data?.pages || 1
  } catch (err) {
    error.value = err.message
    documentos.value = []
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

// 🔥 ACCIÓN DEL BOTÓN Nº: Invierte el orden, vuelve a la página 1 y recarga
const toggleOrden = () => {
  if (loading.value) return
  ordenDescendente.value = !ordenDescendente.value
  page.value = 1
  cargarDocumentos()
  if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })
}

const onSearchInput = () => {
  if (searchTimeout) clearTimeout(searchTimeout)
  searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => { page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }, 500)
}

const limpiarBusqueda = () => { filters.value.search = ''; page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }

const irAPagina = (p) => {
  if (p >= 1 && p <= totalPages.value) { 
    page.value = p
    cargarDocumentos()
    if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }) 
  }
}

const prevPage = () => { if (page.value > 1) irAPagina(page.value - 1) }
const nextPage = () => { if (page.value < totalPages.value) irAPagina(page.value + 1) }

onMounted(() => { cargarDocumentos() })
</script>

<style scoped>
/* 🔥 Aumentado a 5 líneas para mostrar más descripción */
.line-clamp-5 {
  display: -webkit-box;
  -webkit-line-clamp: 5;
  -webkit-box-orient: vertical;
  overflow: hidden;
}
</style>

                  >

                  <span

                  </span>

              />

        <!-- Sin senadores -->

          v-if="titularesDeBancada.length === 0 && suplentesDeBancada.length === 0"
          class="text-center py-12 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100"
        >
          <Icon name="mdi:account-off-outline" class="text-5xl text-gray-300 mb-3" />
          <p class="text-gray-500">No hay senadores en esta bancada</p>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- Botón volver -->
      <div class="text-center mt-12">
        <NuxtLink
          to="/"
          class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[#611717] hover:text-[#3a060d] transition-colors text-sm md:text-base font-medium"
        >
          <Icon name="mdi:arrow-left" class="text-lg" />
          Volver al inicio
        </NuxtLink>
      </div>
    </main>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { senadores } from '~/data/senadores'
import { partidosData } from '~/data/partidos'

useHead({
  title: 'Bancadas | Senado de Bolivia',
  meta: [
    {
      name: 'description',
      content: 'Conoce las bancadas políticas del Senado de Bolivia y los senadores titulares y suplentes que las integran.'
    },
    { property: 'og:title', content: 'Bancadas | Senado de Bolivia' },
    { property: 'og:description', content: 'Fuerzas políticas representadas en la Cámara de Senadores.' },
    { property: 'og:type', content: 'website' }
  ]
})

const defaultAvatar =
  'data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22 width=%22100%22 height=%22100%22%3E%3Ccircle cx=%2250%22 cy=%2250%22 r=%2250%22 fill=%22%23e5e7eb%22/%3E%3Ctext x=%2250%22 y=%2255%22 text-anchor=%22middle%22 fill=%22%239ca3af%22 font-size=%2240%22 font-family=%22sans-serif%22%3E👤%3C/text%3E%3C/svg%3E'

// ============================================
// ESTADO
// ============================================
const bancadaSeleccionada = ref(null)

// ============================================
// COMPUTED
// ============================================
const totalTitulares = computed(
  () => senadores.filter(s => s.tipo === 'titular').length
)

const bancadasConConteo = computed(() => {
  return partidosData
    .map(partido => {
      const titulares = senadores.filter(
        s => s.party === partido.nombre && s.tipo === 'titular'
      ).length
      const suplentes = senadores.filter(
        s => s.party === partido.nombre && s.tipo === 'suplente'
      ).length
      return {
        ...partido,
        totalTitulares: titulares,
        totalSuplentes: suplentes
      }
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.orden - b.orden)
})

const titularesDeBancada = computed(() => {
  if (!bancadaSeleccionada.value) return []
  return senadores
    .filter(s => s.party === bancadaSeleccionada.value.nombre && s.tipo === 'titular')
    .sort((a, b) => a.seatNumber - b.seatNumber)
})

const suplentesDeBancada = computed(() => {
  if (!bancadaSeleccionada.value) return []
  return senadores
    .filter(s => s.party === bancadaSeleccionada.value.nombre && s.tipo === 'suplente')
    .sort((a, b) => a.seatNumber - b.seatNumber)
})

// ============================================
// MÉTODOS
// ============================================
const toggleBancada = (bancada) => {
  if (bancadaSeleccionada.value?.id === bancada.id) {
    bancadaSeleccionada.value = null
  } else {
    bancadaSeleccionada.value = bancada
    if (process.client) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        window.scrollTo({ top: 400, behavior: 'smooth' })
      }, 100)
    }
  }
}

const porcentaje = (cantidad) => {
  if (totalTitulares.value === 0) return 0
  return Math.round((cantidad / totalTitulares.value) * 100)
}
</script>

<style scoped>
.logo-bancada {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}

.logo-bancada :deep(svg) {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  display: block;
}
</style>

<!-- resoluciones-camarales -->
<template>

            </div>

            <button v-if="filters.search" @click="limpiarBusqueda" class="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-4 flex items-center text-gray-400 hover:text-[#611717] transition-colors" title="Limpiar búsqueda">
              <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" /></svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div v-if="!loading && !error" class="mb-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
        Mostrando <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ documentos.length }}</strong> de <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ total }}</strong> resolución{{ total !== 1 ? 'es' : '' }}
        <span v-if="filters.search"> para "<strong>{{ filters.search }}</strong>"</span>
        <span class="ml-2 text-xs text-gray-400">· Orden: {{ ordenDescendente ? 'más reciente primero' : 'más antiguo primero' }}</span>
      </div>

      <div v-if="loading" class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm py-16 text-center">
        <div class="animate-spin rounded-full h-12 w-12 border-b-2 border-[#611717] mx-auto"></div>
        <p class="mt-4 text-gray-600 text-sm">Cargando resoluciones...</p>
      </div>

      <div v-else-if="error" class="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-6 text-center">
        <p class="text-red-700 font-medium text-sm">{{ error }}</p>
        <button @click="cargarDocumentos" class="mt-3 px-4 py-2 bg-[#611717] text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-[#3a060d] text-sm">Reintentar</button>
      </div>

      <div v-else-if="documentos.length === 0" class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm py-16 text-center">
        <div class="text-5xl mb-3">📭</div>
        <h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-800 mb-1">No hay resoluciones</h3>
        <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm">{{ filters.search ? 'Prueba con otros términos de búsqueda.' : 'Aún no hay resoluciones registradas.' }}</p>
      </div>

      <div v-else class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-md border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
        <div class="overflow-x-auto">
          <table class="min-w-full text-sm">
            <thead class="bg-[#611717] text-white">
              <tr>
                <!-- BOTÓN Nº PARA INVERTIR EL ORDEN -->

                  @click="toggleOrden"
                  class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer select-none hover:bg-[#7a1f1f] transition-colors group w-16"
                  title="Click para invertir el orden"
                >
                  <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                    Nº
                    <span class="text-[10px] opacity-80 group-hover:opacity-100">
                      {{ ordenDescendente ? '▼' : '▲' }}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                </th>
                <!-- Columna Código (antes Nº) -->
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Código</th>
                <!-- 🔥 CAMBIO: "Fecha de Petición" a "Fecha" -->
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Fecha</th>
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-left text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Descripción</th>
                <!-- 🔥 CAMBIO: Columna "Proyectista" eliminada -->
                <th class="px-4 py-4 text-center text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap">Adjunto</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody class="divide-y divide-gray-100">
              <tr v-for="(doc, index) in documentos" :key="doc._id" class="hover:bg-[#fbf8f1] transition-colors">
                <!-- Nº secuencial GLOBAL -->
                <td class="px-4 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                  <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#fbf8f1] text-[#611717] font-bold text-xs border border-[#e3d194]">
                    {{ numeroSecuencial(index) }}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <!-- Código (antes Nº) -->
                <td class="px-4 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                  <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#fbf8f1] text-[#611717] font-bold text-xs border border-[#e3d194]">{{ mayusculas(doc.numero) }}</span>
                </td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-gray-700 whitespace-nowrap text-xs">{{ mayusculas(formatearFecha(doc.fechaIngreso)) }}</td>
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-gray-700 min-w-[320px] max-w-md">
                  <div class="line-clamp-3 text-justify text-xs leading-relaxed" :title="mayusculas(doc.asunto)">{{ mayusculas(doc.asunto) }}</div>
                </td>
                <!-- 🔥 CAMBIO: Celda de "Proyectista" eliminada -->
                <td class="px-4 py-4 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                  <a v-if="doc.archivo && doc.archivo.url" :href="doc.archivo.url" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 border-2 border-[#611717] text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#611717] hover:text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all duration-200" title="Ver PDF">
                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" /></svg>
                    Ver PDF
                  </a>
                  <span v-else class="text-gray-300 text-xs">Sin archivo</span>
                </td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div v-if="totalPages > 1" class="px-6 py-5 border-t border-gray-100 flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-4 bg-[#fbf8f1]">
          <div class="text-xs text-gray-600">
            Mostrando registros del <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ (page - 1) * itemsPorPagina + 1 }}</strong> al <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ Math.min(page * itemsPorPagina, total) }}</strong> de un total de <strong class="text-[#611717]">{{ total }}</strong> registros
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button @click="prevPage" :disabled="page === 1" class="p-2 rounded-md text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#e3d194] disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors">
              <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7" /></svg>
            </button>
            <button v-for="p in paginasVisibles" :key="p" @click="irAPagina(p)" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition-colors" :class="page === p ? 'bg-[#611717] text-white shadow-md' : 'hover:bg-[#e3d194] text-[#611717]'">{{ p }}</button>
            <span v-if="totalPages > 5 && page < totalPages - 2" class="px-1 text-[#611717]">...</span>
            <button @click="nextPage" :disabled="page === totalPages" class="p-2 rounded-md text-[#611717] hover:bg-[#e3d194] disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors">
              <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" /></svg>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'

useHead({
  title: 'Resoluciones Camarales | Senado de Bolivia',
  meta: [{ name: 'description', content: 'Registro oficial de resoluciones camarales de la Cámara de Senadores de Bolivia.' }]
})

const config = useRuntimeConfig()
const API_BASE_URL = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

const documentos = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref(null)
const page = ref(1)
const totalPages = ref(1)
const total = ref(0)
const itemsPorPagina = 10
const filters = ref({ search: '' })
let searchTimeout = null

const ordenDescendente = ref(true)

const paginasVisibles = computed(() => {
  const paginas = []
  const rango = 2
  for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages.value; i++) {
    if (i === 1 || i === totalPages.value || (i >= page.value - rango && i <= page.value + rango)) { paginas.push(i) }
  }
  return paginas
})

// 🔥 Nueva función para convertir texto a mayúsculas
const mayusculas = (texto) => {
  if (texto === null || texto === undefined) return ''
  return String(texto).toUpperCase()
}

const formatearFecha = (fecha) => {
  if (!fecha) return '—'
  try {
    const d = new Date(fecha)
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—'
    return d.toLocaleDateString('es-BO', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' })
  } catch { return '—' }
}

// 🔥 Numeración GLOBAL (Igual a Proyectos Aprobados)
const numeroSecuencial = (index) => {
  if (ordenDescendente.value) {
    return total.value - ((page.value - 1) * itemsPorPagina) - index
  }
  return (page.value - 1) * itemsPorPagina + index + 1
}

const cargarDocumentos = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = null
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()
    params.append('page', String(page.value))
    params.append('limit', String(itemsPorPagina))
    if (filters.value.search) params.append('search', filters.value.search)
    params.append('order', ordenDescendente.value ? 'desc' : 'asc')

    const url = `${API_BASE_URL}/legislativos/resolucion-camaral?${params.toString()}`
    const response = await fetch(url)
    const result = await response.json()

    if (!response.ok || !result.success) throw new Error(result.message || 'Error al cargar las resoluciones')

    documentos.value = result.data?.documentos || []
    total.value = result.data?.total || 0
    totalPages.value = result.data?.pages || 1
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error cargando Resoluciones:', err)
    error.value = err.message || 'Error al cargar las resoluciones'
    documentos.value = []
    total.value = 0
    totalPages.value = 1
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

// 🔥 Botón Nº
const toggleOrden = () => {
  if (loading.value) return
  ordenDescendente.value = !ordenDescendente.value
  page.value = 1
  cargarDocumentos()
  if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })
}

const onSearchInput = () => {
  if (searchTimeout) clearTimeout(searchTimeout)
  searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => { page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }, 500)
}

const limpiarBusqueda = () => { filters.value.search = ''; page.value = 1; cargarDocumentos() }

const irAPagina = (p) => {
  if (p >= 1 && p <= totalPages.value) {
    page.value = p
    cargarDocumentos()
    if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })
  }
}

const prevPage = () => { if (page.value > 1) { page.value--; cargarDocumentos(); if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }) } }
const nextPage = () => { if (page.value < totalPages.value) { page.value++; cargarDocumentos(); if (process.client) window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }) } }

onMounted(() => { cargarDocumentos() })
</script>

<style scoped>
.line-clamp-3 { display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 3; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden; }
</style>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'

  return trimestres.find(t => t.key === trimestreActivo.value) || trimestres[0]
})

const seleccionarTrimestre = (key) => {
  trimestreActivo.value = key
}

const trimestres = [
  {
    key: 't1',
    nombre: 'Primer Trimestre 2026',

      avanceFisico: 17.47,

          { codigo: '51', descripcion: '09 Area Legislativa - Personal Eventual CS', fisico: 12.85, financiero: 12.85, presupuesto: 28130098, ejecutado: 3614728.97 },
        ],
        totalFisico: 10.40,
        totalFinanciero: 12.09,
        totalPresupuesto: 30987735,
        totalEjecutado: 3747551.29,

// ============================================
// FORMATEO DE Bs (sin decimales, con sufijo "Bs")
// ============================================
const formatBs = (n) => {
  if (n === undefined || n === null) return 'Bs 0'
  return 'Bs ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('es-BO', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 0
  }).format(n)
}

  title: 'Seguimiento y Evaluación del POA | Senado de Bolivia',
  meta: [
    { name: 'description', content: 'Avance físico y financiero del Plan Operativo Anual (POA) 2026 de la Cámara de Senadores - Seguimiento trimestral.' },
  ],
})
</script>

<script>
export default {
  name: 'SolicitudInformacion',
  data() {
    return {
      preguntasFrecuentes: [
        {
          pregunta: '¿Cuánto tiempo tengo para recibir respuesta a mi solicitud?',
          respuesta: 'La ley establece un plazo máximo de 30 días hábiles para dar respuesta a tu solicitud. Si la información está disponible, el plazo se reduce a 10 días hábiles.',
          abierta: false
        },
        {
          pregunta: '¿Qué información puedo solicitar al Senado?',
          respuesta: 'Puedes solicitar cualquier información pública generada, administrada o en posesión del Senado, con excepción de aquella que tenga carácter reservado o secreto por ley.',
          abierta: false
        },
        {
          pregunta: '¿Necesito abogado para hacer una solicitud?',
          respuesta: 'No, cualquier ciudadano puede realizar una solicitud de información sin necesidad de representación legal. Solo necesitas tu usuario de Ciudadanía Digital.',
          abierta: false
        },
        {
          pregunta: '¿Qué pasa si mi solicitud es denegada?',
          respuesta: 'Tienes derecho a presentar un recurso de impugnación ante la misma institución o ante el Tribunal Superior de Justicia, conforme a la Ley N° 974.',
          abierta: false
        },
        {
          pregunta: '¿Cómo sé que mi solicitud fue recibida?',
          respuesta: 'El sistema te genera un número de seguimiento y te envía una confirmación a tu correo electrónico registrado.',
          abierta: false
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  methods: {
    toggleFaq(index) {
      this.preguntasFrecuentes[index].abierta = !this.preguntasFrecuentes[index].abierta
    }
  }
}
</script>

    <Teleport to="body">
      <transition
        enter-active-class="transition-opacity duration-300 ease-out"
        enter-from-class="opacity-0"
        enter-to-class="opacity-100"
        leave-active-class="transition-opacity duration-200 ease-in"
        leave-from-class="opacity-100"
        leave-to-class="opacity-0"
      >
        <div 
          v-if="lightboxVisible"
          class="fixed inset-0 z-[999999] bg-black/90 flex items-center justify-center p-[4vw] sm:p-[2vw]"
          @click="cerrarLightbox"
        >
          <div class="relative max-w-[95vw] sm:max-w-[90vw] max-h-[90vh] flex items-center justify-center" @click.stop>
            <div class="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[1.5vw] sm:rounded-[0.5vw]">
              <img 
                ref="lightboxImgRef"
                :src="lightboxImage" 
                :alt="lightboxAlt"
                class="max-w-[90vw] sm:max-w-[85vw] max-h-[75vh] sm:max-h-[80vh] object-contain transition-transform duration-300 ease-out"
                :style="{ transform: `scale(${zoomLevel})`, cursor: zoomLevel > 1 ? 'grab' : 'default' }"
                @wheel="manejarZoom"
                @mousedown="iniciarArrastre"
                @mousemove="moverArrastre"
                @mouseup="terminarArrastre"
                @mouseleave="terminarArrastre"
              />
            </div>
            
            <div class="absolute bottom-[2vw] sm:bottom-[1vw] left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 flex items-center gap-[2vw] sm:gap-4 bg-black/60 rounded-full px-[4vw] sm:px-[1.5vw] py-[1.5vw] sm:py-[0.6vw]">
              <span class="text-white text-[2.5vw] sm:text-[0.9vw] font-medium max-w-[40vw] sm:max-w-[30vw] truncate">{{ lightboxAlt }}</span>
              
              <div class="w-[0.2vw] sm:w-[0.1vw] h-[3vw] sm:h-[1.5vw] bg-white/30"></div>
              
              <button 
                @click="zoomOut"
                class="text-white hover:text-gray-300 transition-colors text-[4vw] sm:text-[1.2vw] w-[5vw] sm:w-[2vw] h-[5vw] sm:h-[2vw] flex items-center justify-center"
                title="Alejar"
              >
                <svg class="w-[4vw] sm:w-[1.2vw] h-[4vw] sm:h-[1.2vw]" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0zM13 10H7" />
                </svg>
              </button>
              
              <span class="text-white text-[2.5vw] sm:text-[0.8vw] font-mono min-w-[6vw] sm:min-w-[3vw] text-center">{{ Math.round(zoomLevel * 100) }}%</span>
              
              <button 
                @click="zoomIn"
                class="text-white hover:text-gray-300 transition-colors text-[4vw] sm:text-[1.2vw] w-[5vw] sm:w-[2vw] h-[5vw] sm:h-[2vw] flex items-center justify-center"
                title="Acercar"
              >
                <svg class="w-[4vw] sm:w-[1.2vw] h-[4vw] sm:h-[1.2vw]" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0zM10 7v6m-3-3h6" />
                </svg>
              </button>
              
              <div class="w-[0.2vw] sm:w-[0.1vw] h-[3vw] sm:h-[1.5vw] bg-white/30"></div>
              
              <button 
                @click="resetearZoom"
                class="text-white/60 hover:text-white transition-colors text-[2.5vw] sm:text-[0.8vw] px-[2vw] sm:px-[0.8vw] py-[1vw] sm:py-[0.3vw] rounded-[1vw] sm:rounded-[0.3vw] hover:bg-white/10"
                title="Restablecer zoom"
              >
                Reset
              </button>
              
              <button 
                @click="cerrarLightbox"
                class="text-white hover:text-gray-300 transition-colors text-[4vw] sm:text-[1.2vw] w-[5vw] sm:w-[2vw] h-[5vw] sm:h-[2vw] flex items-center justify-center"
                title="Cerrar (ESC)"
              >
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </transition>
    </Teleport>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, onBeforeUnmount } from 'vue'

// ============================================
// LIGHTBOX STATE
// ============================================
const lightboxVisible = ref(false)
const lightboxImage = ref('')
const lightboxAlt = ref('')
const zoomLevel = ref(1)
const lightboxImgRef = ref(null)

const isDragging = ref(false)
const dragStartX = ref(0)
const dragStartY = ref(0)
const dragOffsetX = ref(0)
const dragOffsetY = ref(0)
const currentTranslateX = ref(0)
const currentTranslateY = ref(0)

// ============================================
// FUNCIONES DEL LIGHTBOX
// ============================================
const abrirLightbox = (src, alt) => {
  lightboxImage.value = src
  lightboxAlt.value = alt
  lightboxVisible.value = true
  zoomLevel.value = 1
  currentTranslateX.value = 0
  currentTranslateY.value = 0
  document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'
}

const cerrarLightbox = () => {
  lightboxVisible.value = false
  document.body.style.overflow = 'auto'
  zoomLevel.value = 1
  currentTranslateX.value = 0
  currentTranslateY.value = 0
}

// ============================================
// ZOOM
// ============================================
const zoomIn = () => {
  zoomLevel.value = Math.min(zoomLevel.value + 0.25, 3)
  actualizarTransformacion()
}

const zoomOut = () => {
  zoomLevel.value = Math.max(zoomLevel.value - 0.25, 0.5)
  actualizarTransformacion()
}

const resetearZoom = () => {
  zoomLevel.value = 1
  currentTranslateX.value = 0
  currentTranslateY.value = 0
  actualizarTransformacion()
}

const manejarZoom = (event) => {
  event.preventDefault()
  const delta = event.deltaY > 0 ? -0.15 : 0.15
  zoomLevel.value = Math.min(Math.max(zoomLevel.value + delta, 0.5), 3)
  actualizarTransformacion()
}

const actualizarTransformacion = () => {
  if (lightboxImgRef.value) {
    lightboxImgRef.value.style.transform = `scale(${zoomLevel.value}) translate(${currentTranslateX.value}px, ${currentTranslateY.value}px)`
  }
}

// ============================================
// ARRASTRE
// ============================================
const iniciarArrastre = (event) => {
  if (zoomLevel.value <= 1) return
  isDragging.value = true
  dragStartX.value = event.clientX - dragOffsetX.value
  dragStartY.value = event.clientY - dragOffsetY.value
  if (lightboxImgRef.value) {
    lightboxImgRef.value.style.cursor = 'grabbing'
  }
}

const moverArrastre = (event) => {
  if (!isDragging.value || zoomLevel.value <= 1) return
  dragOffsetX.value = event.clientX - dragStartX.value
  dragOffsetY.value = event.clientY - dragStartY.value
  const maxOffset = 200 * zoomLevel.value
  currentTranslateX.value = Math.min(Math.max(dragOffsetX.value, -maxOffset), maxOffset)
  currentTranslateY.value = Math.min(Math.max(dragOffsetY.value, -maxOffset), maxOffset)
  actualizarTransformacion()
}

const terminarArrastre = () => {
  isDragging.value = false
  if (lightboxImgRef.value) {
    lightboxImgRef.value.style.cursor = zoomLevel.value > 1 ? 'grab' : 'default'
  }
}

// ============================================
// TECLADO
// ============================================
const handleKeydown = (e) => {
  if (!lightboxVisible.value) return
  if (e.key === 'Escape') cerrarLightbox()
  if (e.key === '+' || e.key === '=') zoomIn()
  if (e.key === '-') zoomOut()
  if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') resetearZoom()
}

onMounted(() => {
  document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown)
})

onBeforeUnmount(() => {
  document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown)
})
</script>

        <div class="text-[10vw] sm:text-4xl mb-3">⚠️</div>
        <p class="text-red-600 font-medium text-[3.6vw] sm:text-base">{{ error }}</p>
        <button @click="recargar" class="mt-4 px-6 py-2 bg-senado-primary text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-senado-primary-dark transition text-sm">
          Reintentar
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- Contenido -->
      <template v-else>
        <!-- Tabs de navegación -->
        <div
          class="grid gap-[2.4vw] sm:gap-4 mb-[4.5vw] sm:mb-6"
          :class="tabs.length === 3 ? 'grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3' : 'grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2'"
        >
          <button
            v-for="tab in tabs"
            :key="tab.key"
            @click="tabActivo = tab.key"
            class="relative rounded-xl transition-all duration-300 text-left overflow-hidden group px-[2.4vw] sm:px-5 py-[2.4vw] sm:py-4 shadow-sm border border-gray-200"
            :class="tabActivo === tab.key
              ? 'bg-[#5e0d0d] border-[#5e0d0d] shadow-lg' 
              : 'bg-white hover:shadow-md'"
          >
            <div class="relative z-10 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-[1.8vw] sm:gap-3">
                <!-- Ícono -->
                <div
                  class="rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-300 w-[6.6vw] sm:w-10 h-[6.6vw] sm:h-10 flex-shrink-0"
                  :class="tabActivo === tab.key
                    ? 'bg-white/10 text-senado-gold'
                    : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 group-hover:bg-gray-200'"
                >
                  <Icon :name="tab.icono" class="text-[3.6vw] sm:text-xl" />
                </div>

                <!-- Textos -->
                <div>
                  <h3
                    class="font-semibold transition-colors duration-300 text-[2.55vw] sm:text-[0.95rem] leading-tight"
                    :class="tabActivo === tab.key ? 'text-senado-gold' : 'text-gray-800'"
                  >
                    {{ tab.nombre }}
                  </h3>
                  <div class="flex items-center gap-[1.5vw] sm:gap-2 mt-[0.5vw] sm:mt-1">
                    <span
                      class="font-bold text-[3.6vw] sm:text-xl"
                      :class="tabActivo === tab.key ? 'text-senado-gold' : 'text-gray-500'"
                    >
                      {{ obtenerTotalPorTipo(tab.key) }}
                    </span>
                    <span 
                      class="text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs"
                      :class="tabActivo === tab.key ? 'text-white/70' : 'text-gray-400'"
                    >
                      documentos
                    </span>

                    <!-- Rango de años SIEMPRE visible, con estilos dinámicos -->
                    <span
                      class="px-[1.5vw] sm:px-2 py-[0.15vw] sm:py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold text-[1.35vw] sm:text-[10px] tracking-wider transition-colors duration-300"
                      :class="tabActivo === tab.key 
                        ? 'bg-white/10 text-white' 
                        : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500'"
                    >
                      {{ obtenerRangoAnios(tab.key) }}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <!-- Flecha -->
              <div
                class="transition-all duration-300 text-[3.9vw] sm:text-xl"
                :class="tabActivo === tab.key ? 'text-senado-gold opacity-100' : 'text-gray-300 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100'"
              >
                <Icon name="mdi:chevron-right" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </button>
        </div>

        <!-- Contenido de los tabs -->
        <div v-for="tab in tabs" :key="tab.key">
          <div v-show="tabActivo === tab.key">
            
            <!-- Tabla de documentos -->
            <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden border border-gray-200">
              <div class="border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between px-[3.6vw] sm:px-4 py-[1.8vw] sm:py-2">
                <h2 class="font-bold text-senado-primary flex items-center gap-[1.2vw] sm:gap-2 text-[3.3vw] sm:text-base">
                  <Icon :name="tab.icono" class="text-[4.2vw] sm:text-xl" />
                  {{ tab.nombre }}
                </h2>
                <span class="text-gray-400 flex items-center gap-[0.6vw] sm:gap-1 text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs">
                  Ordenados: más reciente a más antiguo
                  <Icon name="mdi:arrow-down" class="text-[2.1vw] sm:text-sm" />
                </span>
              </div>

              <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                <table class="w-full">
                  <thead>
                    <tr class="bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-200">
                      <th class="text-left text-gray-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider px-[2.4vw] sm:px-3 py-[1.5vw] sm:py-2 text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs">Codigo</th>
                      <th class="text-left text-gray-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider px-[2.4vw] sm:px-3 py-[1.5vw] sm:py-2 text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs">Gestión</th>
                      <th class="text-left text-gray-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider px-[2.4vw] sm:px-3 py-[1.5vw] sm:py-2 text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs">Descripción</th>
                      <th class="text-center text-gray-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider px-[2.4vw] sm:px-3 py-[1.5vw] sm:py-2 text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs">Archivo</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    <tr
                      v-for="(item, index) in obtenerDocumentosOrdenados(tab.key)"
                      :key="item.id || index"
                      class="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      @click="verPDF(item)"
                    >
                      <td class="px-[2.4vw] sm:px-3 py-[1.5vw] sm:py-2 min-w-[30vw] sm:min-w-[220px]">
                        <span class="font-mono font-medium text-senado-primary text-[2.7vw] sm:text-sm">{{ item.titulo }}</span>
                      </td>
                      <td class="px-[2.4vw] sm:px-3 py-[1.5vw] sm:py-2">
                        <span class="font-bold text-[2.7vw] sm:text-sm">{{ item.gestion }}</span>
                      </td>
                      <td class="px-[2.4vw] sm:px-3 py-[1.5vw] sm:py-2">
                        <span class="text-[2.55vw] sm:text-sm text-gray-700">{{ item.descripcion }}</span>
                      </td>
                      <td class="px-[2.4vw] sm:px-3 py-[1.5vw] sm:py-2">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-[1.8vw] sm:gap-3">
                          <a
                            v-if="item.url"
                            :href="item.url"
                            target="_blank"
                            class="bg-senado-primary text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-senado-primary-dark transition-colors inline-flex items-center font-medium px-[2.4vw] sm:px-3 py-[1.2vw] sm:py-1.5 text-[2.1vw] sm:text-xs gap-[0.9vw] sm:gap-1.5"
                            @click.stop
                          >
                            <Icon name="mdi:download" class="text-[2.7vw] sm:text-sm" />
                            Descargar
                          </a>
                          <span v-else class="text-gray-300 text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs">Sin PDF</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                    <!-- Sin datos -->
                    <tr v-if="obtenerDocumentosOrdenados(tab.key).length === 0">
                      <td colspan="4" class="text-center py-[4vw] sm:py-8 text-gray-500 text-[2.7vw] sm:text-sm">
                        No hay documentos disponibles para {{ tab.nombre }}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Leyenda -->
        <div class="mt-[6vw] sm:mt-8 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 px-[3vw] sm:px-5 py-[2.4vw] sm:py-3">
          <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-[3vw] sm:gap-4">
            <span class="font-semibold text-gray-700 text-[2.1vw] sm:text-sm">Tipos de Auditoría:</span>
            <span class="flex items-center gap-[1.2vw] sm:gap-2">
              <span class="rounded-full w-[2.1vw] sm:w-2.5 h-[2.1vw] sm:h-2.5 bg-blue-500"></span>
              <span class="text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs text-gray-600">Cumplimiento</span>
            </span>
            <span class="flex items-center gap-[1.2vw] sm:gap-2">
              <span class="rounded-full w-[2.1vw] sm:w-2.5 h-[2.1vw] sm:h-2.5 bg-green-500"></span>
              <span class="text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs text-gray-600">Operacionales</span>
            </span>
            <span class="flex items-center gap-[1.2vw] sm:gap-2">
              <span class="rounded-full w-[2.1vw] sm:w-2.5 h-[2.1vw] sm:h-2.5 bg-purple-500"></span>
              <span class="text-[1.8vw] sm:text-xs text-gray-600">Confiabilidad</span>
            </span>

              <span class="text-[1.65vw] sm:text-xs text-gray-400">💡 Haz clic en cualquier fila para ver el PDF</span>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'

// ============================================
// CONFIGURACIÓN
// ============================================
const API_BASE_URL = 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'
const MODULO = 'auditorias-ejecutadas'

// ============================================
// ESTADO
// ============================================
const categorias = ref([])
const documentos = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref(null)
const tabActivo = ref('')

// ============================================
// COMPUTED
// ============================================
const tabs = computed(() => {
  if (categorias.value.length === 0) {
    // Fallback mientras carga
    return [
      { key: 'confiabilidad', nombre: 'Auditorías de Confiabilidad', icono: 'mdi:shield-check' },
      { key: 'cumplimiento',  nombre: 'Auditorías de Cumplimiento', icono: 'mdi:clipboard-check' },
      { key: 'operacionales', nombre: 'Auditorías de Operacionales',    icono: 'mdi:cog' },
    ]
  }
  const lista = [...categorias.value]
  const idx = lista.findIndex(c => c.key === 'confiabilidad')
  if (idx > 0) {
    const [confiabilidad] = lista.splice(idx, 1)
    lista.unshift(confiabilidad)
  }
  return lista
})

const totalAuditorias = computed(() => documentos.value.length)

const documentosPorTipo = computed(() => {
  const agrupados = {}
  documentos.value.forEach(doc => {
    if (!agrupados[doc.categoria]) {
      agrupados[doc.categoria] = []
    }
    agrupados[doc.categoria].push(doc)
  })
  return agrupados
})

// ============================================
// MÉTODOS
// ============================================
const obtenerDocumentosOrdenados = (tipo) => {
  const docs = documentosPorTipo.value[tipo] || []
  return [...docs].sort((a, b) => b.gestion - a.gestion)
}

const obtenerTotalPorTipo = (tipo) => {
  return documentosPorTipo.value[tipo]?.length || 0
}

const obtenerUltimoAnio = (tipo) => {
  const docs = documentosPorTipo.value[tipo] || []
  if (docs.length === 0) return '-'
  return Math.max(...docs.map(d => d.gestion))
}

const obtenerAniosUnicos = (tipo) => {
  const docs = documentosPorTipo.value[tipo] || []
  return new Set(docs.map(d => d.gestion)).size
}

const obtenerRangoAnios = (tipo) => {
  const docs = documentosPorTipo.value[tipo] || []
  if (docs.length === 0) return '-'
  const anios = docs.map(d => d.gestion)
  const min = Math.min(...anios)
  const max = Math.max(...anios)
  return min === max ? `${min}` : `${min} - ${max}`
}

const verPDF = (item) => {
  if (item.url) {
    window.open(item.url, '_blank')
  } else {
    alert(`📄 ${item.titulo}\n\n${item.descripcion}\n\n⚠️ No hay PDF disponible.`)
  }
}

const cargarDatos = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = null

  try {
    const controller = new AbortController()
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 10000)

    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/auditoria/${MODULO}`, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' }
    })
    clearTimeout(timeoutId)

    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Error ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`)

    const result = await response.json()

    if (result.success && result.data) {
      
      // 🔥 FORZAMOS LOS NOMBRES EXACTOS QUE SOLICITASTE
      if (result.data.categorias && result.data.categorias.length > 0) {
        const nombresPersonalizados = {
          'confiabilidad': 'Auditorías de Confiabilidad',
          'cumplimiento': 'Auditorías de Cumplimiento',
          'operacionales': 'Auditorías de Operacionales'
        }
        
        categorias.value = result.data.categorias.map(cat => ({
          ...cat,
          nombre: nombresPersonalizados[cat.key] || cat.nombre
        }))
      } else {
        categorias.value = []
      }

      documentos.value = result.data.documentos || []

      // Activar el primer tab disponible
      if (categorias.value.length > 0) {
        tabActivo.value = categorias.value[0].key
      }

      console.log(`✅ [Auditorías Ejecutadas] ${categorias.value.length} categorías, ${documentos.value.length} documentos`)
    } else {
      throw new Error(result.message || 'Error al cargar datos')
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name === 'AbortError') {
      error.value = 'La solicitud tardó demasiado. Intente nuevamente.'
    } else {
      error.value = err.message
    }
    console.error('❌ [Auditorías Ejecutadas] Error:', err)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const recargar = () => {
  cargarDatos()
}

// ============================================
// LIFECYCLE
// ============================================
onMounted(() => {
  cargarDatos()
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.container {
  max-width: 1200px;
  margin-left: auto;
  margin-right: auto;
}

.hover\:bg-gray-50\/50:hover {
  background-color: rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.5);
}

tbody tr {
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: background-color 0.2s ease;
}

tbody tr:hover {
  background-color: #f9fafb;
}

.transform {
  transition: transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;
}

.group:hover .transform {
  transform: scale(1.02);
}
</style>
